# Colony phase: fuel for the metropole, the trade concession and the
# gendarmerie's sweeps against Weber's network. The category closes at the
# Vorkerland collapse; running work settles through its cancel trigger.
NAM_colony = {
	NAM_colonial_rifle_contract = {
		icon = ADISCORD_arms_supply
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_colony_phase = yes
			has_completed_focus = NAM_deliver_the_quota
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_colonial_programme_operable_tt
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 30
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 60
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_result_tt
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid }
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
				}
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 4500
					producer = NAM
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 40
					producer = NAM
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid } }
					hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid }
					add_political_power = 30
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid } }
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_rifle_contract_paid }
				add_political_power = 30
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 5 }
	}

	NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve = {
		icon = oil
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_colony_phase = yes
			has_completed_focus = NAM_concealed_reserve
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_colonial_programme_operable_tt
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_hidden_fuel_capacity_tt
				NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hidden_fuel > 60000 } }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
			NOT = { has_fuel < 40000 }
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 60
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_paid }
						NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
						NOT = { has_fuel < 40000 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hidden_fuel > 60000 } }
					}
					add_political_power = -25
					add_fuel = -40000
					set_country_flag = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_paid
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = { NAM_colonial_programme_operable = no }
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_paid }
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_delivered_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_paid
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_hidden_fuel value = 40000 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				hidden_effect = { NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_refund = yes }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_colonial_fuel_reserve_refund = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 10 }
	}

	NAM_colonial_workshops = {
		icon = generic_industry
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_colony_phase = yes
			has_completed_focus = NAM_wrk_return_cargo
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_colonial_programme_operable_tt
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
			}
			owns_state = 700
			controls_state = 700
			700 = {
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
		}
		cost = 60
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 35
		days_re_enable = 120
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_workshops_result_tt
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
					owns_state = 700
					controls_state = 700
					700 = {
						free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid }
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
					owns_state = 700
					controls_state = 700
					700 = {
						free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid }
				700 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid } }
					hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid }
					add_political_power = 60
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid } }
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_workshops_paid }
				add_political_power = 60
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 5 }
	}

	NAM_colonial_artillery_contract = {
		icon = ADISCORD_arms_supply
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_colony_phase = yes
			has_completed_focus = NAM_metropolitan_depots
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_colonial_programme_operable_tt
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 450 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 35
		days_re_enable = 90
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_result_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
						has_completed_focus = NAM_metropolitan_depots
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_paid }
						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 450 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_country_flag = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_paid
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 450 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_colonial_programme_ended_tt
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = no
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_paid }
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
				}
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_paid }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 240
					producer = NAM
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 160
					producer = NAM
				}
			}
			else = { NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_refund = yes }
		}
		cancel_effect = { NAM_colonial_artillery_contract_refund = yes }
		ai_will_do = { base = 5 }
	}

		}
		cost = 35

		days_remove = 14
		days_re_enable = 60
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_result_tt
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
					NAM_underground_active = yes
					check_variable = { var = NAM_underground value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid }
					NAM_colonial_programme_operable = yes
					NAM_underground_active = yes
					check_variable = { var = NAM_underground value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				}
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_colonial_gendarme_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_underground_change value = -15 }
					NAM_underground_add = yes
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -3 }
					NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid } }
					hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid }
					add_political_power = 35
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid } }
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_colonial_gendarme_operation_paid }
				add_political_power = 35
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 5 }
	}

		icon = ADISCORD_arms_supply
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {

	# Negotiations take 21 days and hold the treasury price in escrow; the

		}

			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_wrk_concession_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_wrk_concession_refund = yes }
		}

			base = 15

	# The gendarmerie combs one district for Weber's cells. One sweep at a
	# time; a district can be swept again after 120 days.
	NAM_counterinsurgency_sweep = {
		icon = generic_operation
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 67 688 689 690 719 700 }
		target_trigger = { FROM = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
		highlight_states = { highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM } }
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		visible = {
			NAM_colony_phase = yes
			has_completed_focus = NAM_colonial_watch
			NAM_underground_open = yes
			FROM = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_swept }
			}
		}
		available = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_underground_left_tt
				check_variable = { NAM_underground > 0 }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_sweep_slot_free_tt
				NAM_sweep_slot_free = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_cost_counterinsurgency_sweep
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 21
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_country_operable = no
				NAM_colony_phase = no
				FROM = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
				FROM = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_sweep_slot_free = yes
						NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_sweep_in_progress } }
					}
				}
				add_political_power = -20
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_sweep_started_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 75 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
					set_country_flag = NAM_sweep_running
					FROM = {
						set_state_flag = NAM_sweep_in_progress
						set_variable = { var = NAM_sweep_deposit value = 75 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_sweep_done_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { NAM_sweep_complete = yes }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_sweep_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { NAM_sweep_release = yes }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 4
				check_variable = { NAM_underground > 39 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = { state = 688 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

					}
				}
				add_political_power = -25

		}
		cancel_trigger = {

				AND = {
					NAM_independent_programmes_operable = yes
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {

						NAM_independent_programmes_operable = yes
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_delivered_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					clr_country_flag = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_paid
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_oil_support value = 12 }
					NAM_groups_refresh = yes
					add_fuel = 6000
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = {
							has_country_flag = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_paid
						}
					}
					add_political_power = 25
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_refund_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						clr_country_flag = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_paid
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
						NAM_add_treasury = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_country_flag = NAM_council_fuel_reserve_paid
					}
				}
				add_political_power = 25

			NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 35
		days_re_enable = 120
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_independent_programmes_operable = yes
						689 = {
							is_owned_by = NAM
							is_controlled_by = NAM
							infrastructure < 5
						}
						NOT = { has_political_power < 25 }
						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						has_completed_focus = NAM_open_ledgers
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid }
					}
				}
				add_political_power = -25
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_workshop_contract_result_tt
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					NAM_independent_programmes_operable = yes
					689 = {
						is_owned_by = NAM
						is_controlled_by = NAM
						infrastructure < 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
						NAM_independent_programmes_operable = yes
						689 = {
							is_owned_by = NAM
							is_controlled_by = NAM
							infrastructure < 5
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_workshop_contract_delivered_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					clr_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 12 }
					NAM_groups_refresh = yes
					689 = {
						add_building_construction = {
							type = infrastructure
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
					ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = {
							has_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
						}
					}
					add_political_power = 25
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_workshop_contract_refund_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						clr_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
						NAM_add_treasury = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
					}
				}
				add_political_power = 25
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_council_workshop_contract_refund_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					clr_country_flag = NAM_council_workshop_contract_paid
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
					NAM_add_treasury = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 8 }

							check_variable = { var = NAM_project_deposit value = 200 compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = NAM_project_pp_deposit value = 30 compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_econ_deep_drilling_delivered_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = { FROM = { NAM_artel_survey_open = yes } }
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_artel_survey_tt
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					FROM = {
						NAM_project_close = yes
						if = {
							limit = {
								NAM_artel_survey_open = yes
							}
							add_to_variable = { var = NAM_field_max_capacity value = 2 }
							set_state_flag = NAM_artel_surveyed
						}
						add_to_variable = { var = NAM_field_capacity value = 2 }
						clamp_variable = { var = NAM_field_capacity min = 0 max = NAM_field_max_capacity }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NAM_agreement_oil_active = yes }
						add_to_variable = { var = NAM_agreement_progress value = 1 }
					}
					NAM_oil_refresh = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = {
							FROM = {
								has_state_flag = NAM_project_in_progress
								check_variable = { var = NAM_project_deposit value = 200 compare = equals }
								check_variable = { var = NAM_project_pp_deposit value = 30 compare = equals }
							}
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_econ_deep_drilling_refund_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						FROM = { NAM_project_release = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						FROM = {
							has_state_flag = NAM_project_in_progress
							check_variable = { var = NAM_project_deposit value = 200 compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = NAM_project_pp_deposit value = 30 compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_econ_deep_drilling_refund_tt

				}

		}

						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				add_political_power = -35
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 500 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { NAM_housing_series_available = yes }
						set_country_flag = { flag = NAM_veteran_housing_paid value = 2 }
					}
					else = { set_country_flag = NAM_veteran_housing_paid }
					set_country_flag = NAM_pw_civil_busy
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_veteran_housing_result_tt
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { NAM_housing_series_available = yes } }
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_veteran_housing_series_tt
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = { NAM_veteran_housing_ready = no }
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_veteran_housing_result_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_veteran_housing_settle = yes }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pw_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_veteran_housing_refund = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 8 }
	}

					}
				}

					NAM_spend_treasury = yes

				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_island_public_works_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {

				}
			}

						}
					}
				}
			}

							NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_pw_civil_court_awards } }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_pw_district_title_books } }
						add_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = NAM_pw_civil_court_awards
							days = 180
						}
					}
					else = {
						add_dynamic_modifier = {
							modifier = NAM_pw_civil_court_awards
							days = 90
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NAM_border_integration_site = yes
							has_resistance = yes
						}
						add_resistance = -5
						if = {
							limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_pw_district_title_books } }
							add_compliance = 5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 4
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_political_power < 100
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = {
					NOT = { resistance > 5 }
					NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_pw_district_title_books } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

				tooltip = NAM_pw_civil_free_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_pw_civil_busy }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_program_tool_reserve_cost
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury
				value = 900
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 90
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_completed_focus = NAM_pw_tool_reserve
						NAM_program_tool_reserve_ready = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_pw_civil_busy }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_program_tool_reserve_paid }
						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 900 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = {
						var = NAM_treasury_amount
						value = 900
					}
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
					set_country_flag = NAM_program_tool_reserve_paid
					set_country_flag = NAM_pw_civil_busy
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_program_tool_reserve_result_tt
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_program_cannot_finish_tt
				NAM_program_tool_reserve_ready = no
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_program_tool_reserve_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				NAM_program_tool_reserve_settle = yes
			}
		}

			}

				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_program_air_supply_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {

					NAM_spend_treasury = yes

					}

					FROM = {

						add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = NAM_air_bridge_deliveries }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_air_bridge_lost_tt
				OR = {
					NAM_air_bridge_departure = no
					FROM = { NAM_air_bridge_destination = no }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_air_bridge_end_tt
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { NAM_air_bridge_close = yes } }
		}
		remove_effect = { hidden_effect = { FROM = { NAM_air_bridge_close = yes } } }
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 4
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_war = no
					FROM = { NOT = { any_neighbor_state = { controller = { has_war_with = NAM } } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Svetlogorye's operations in the shared War for the South category. They open
# only while Svetlogorye leads the northern side of the running campaign. The
# post-war tree chooses one of them; the defeat tree's Southern Compact, which
# starts that war itself, opens both.
ADISCORD_south_final_war_category = {
	ADISCORD_south_preparation = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = NAM
				tag = SHL
				tag = EFL
				tag = AZH
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = no
		is_good = yes
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_started
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_final_resolved
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_south_preparation_started }
			}
		}
		timeout_effect = { }
	}

	NAM_south_allied_fuel = {
		icon = oil
		allowed = { tag = NAM }

					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 200 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
					set_variable = { var = NAM_weber_talks_deposit value = 200 }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_weber_talks_possible = no
				check_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 50 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_weber_talks_deposit value = 200 compare = equals } } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_weber_talks_3_cancel_tt
				hidden_effect = { NAM_weber_talks_refund = yes }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = NAM_weber_talks_deposit value = 200 compare = equals } } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = {
							NAM_weber_talks_possible = yes
							has_country_flag = NAM_weber_talks_2_done
							check_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_weber_amnesty_tt
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_completed_focus = NAM_mine_councils
							NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_amnesty_recruits_delivered }
						}
						effect_tooltip = { add_manpower = 2000 }
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						clear_variable = NAM_weber_talks_deposit
						NAM_weber_amnesty = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_weber_talks_3_cancel_tt
					hidden_effect = { NAM_weber_talks_refund = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
		}
	}

	# The vertical's answer: search a district for the network's arms caches.
	NAM_hunt_caches = {
		icon = generic_operation
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		state_target = yes
		targets = { 67 688 689 690 719 700 }
		target_trigger = { FROM = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
		highlight_states = { highlight_state_targets = { state = FROM } }
		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = NAM_cache_hunters
			NAM_underground_open = yes
			FROM = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
			}
		}
		available = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_underground_left_tt
				check_variable = { NAM_underground > 0 }
			}
			FROM = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = NAM_caches_not_searched_tt
					NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_caches_searched }
				}
			}
		}
		cost = 30
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 30
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_country_operable = yes
						NAM_underground_open = yes
						has_completed_focus = NAM_cache_hunters
						check_variable = { NAM_underground > 0 }
						FROM = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
							NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_caches_searched }
						}
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 4500
					producer = NAM
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hunt_caches_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					FROM = {
						set_state_flag = {
							flag = NAM_caches_searched
							value = 1
							days = 180
						}
					}
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_underground value = -10 }
					NAM_underground_clamp = yes
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -5 }
					NAM_groups_refresh = yes
				}
			}
			else = { add_political_power = 30 }
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 4
				check_variable = { NAM_underground > 39 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Island exile: the flotillas live from the sea, the black market turns the
# loot into arms and the exiles drill for the return.
NAM_piracy = {
	# A flotilla raids a foreign shipping lane that passes the islands for 15
	# days; tools/builders/build_adiscord_nam_piracy_map.py keeps every lane
	# within reach. The lane's owners are its victims (NAM_pirate_lane_<id>_open
	# and NAM_pirate_save_lane_victims). The fuel is burnt at sea; an
	# interrupted raid returns it and frees the flotilla. The country flag
	# NAM_pirate_at_sea_<id> is the receipt of a paid raid.
	# Azhar's tankers carry oil to Eflor's bay past the eastern cape of
	# the main island.
	NAM_pirate_raid_lane_oil = {
		icon = generic_naval
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_pirate_commissioned = yes
			NAM_pirate_lane_oil_open = yes
		}
		available = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_pirate_slot_free_tt
				NAM_pirate_slot_free = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_pirate_fleet_tt
				has_navy_size = { size > 0 }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_pirate_not_lying_low_tt
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_pirate_lying_low }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_cost_pirate_raid
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 10 }
			NOT = { has_fuel < 1000 }
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 15
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_country_operable = no
				NAM_pirate_commissioned = no
				NAM_pirate_lane_oil_open = no
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_pirate_raid_ready = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil }
						NOT = { has_political_power < 10 }
						NOT = { has_fuel < 1000 }
						NAM_pirate_lane_oil_open = yes
					}
				}
				add_political_power = -10
				add_fuel = -1000
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_raid_started_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_raids_active value = 1 }
					set_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_lane_oil_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil }
					clr_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil
					if = {
						limit = { NAM_pirate_commissioned = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { NAM_pirate_lane_input = 1 }
						NAM_pirate_raid_settle = yes
					}
					else = { NAM_pirate_raid_release = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_pirate_raid_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil }
					clr_country_flag = NAM_pirate_at_sea_oil
					NAM_pirate_raid_release = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = { NAM_pirate_notoriety > 69 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_fuel < 4000
			}
		}
	}

# Island exile: the landing drill both courses share once the island council
# of event .100 has chosen the course.
NAM_exile_politics = {
	# Thirty days of landing drill on the island beaches. The junta pays 30
	# loot, the humanists 30 corporate capital; the deposit returns in the
	# same currency if the drill is interrupted. The piracy window starts the
	# junta's drill through activate_decision.
	NAM_exile_drill = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_exile_return_programme_open = yes
			NAM_exile_drill_unlocked = yes
		}
		available = {
			NAM_country_operable = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_revenge_not_full_tt
				check_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = 100 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		custom_cost_text = NAM_cost_exile_drill
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
			NAM_exile_drill_currency_ready = yes
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 30
		days_re_enable = 15
		cancel_trigger = {
			NAM_country_operable = no
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NOT = { has_political_power < 20 }
						NAM_exile_drill_currency_ready = yes
					}
				}
				add_political_power = -20
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_exile_drill_started_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_exile_humanist }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -30 }
						NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_pirate_loot_change value = -30 }
						NAM_pirate_loot_add = yes
					}
					set_variable = { var = NAM_exile_drill_deposit value = 30 }
					set_country_flag = { flag = NAM_exile_drill_cd value = 1 days = 45 }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_exile_drill_deposit value = 0 compare = greater_than }
					}
				}
				army_experience = 15
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_exile_drill_done_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					clear_variable = NAM_exile_drill_deposit
					add_to_variable = { var = NAM_revenge_readiness value = 10 }
					NAM_revenge_readiness_clamp = yes
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_exile_drill_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_exile_drill_refund = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
	}

	# The exile war ends only in full victory or in the annexation of
	# Svetlogorye, except when NAM_war_of_return_close_external closes it
	# outside the treaty: its principals were removed or made peace by another
	# hand. The course's war focus is spent, so this decision declares the next
	# exile war on Eflor and Azhar; visible only in that case, because a won
	# war closes the return programme.
	NAM_exile_renew_war_of_return = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			has_global_flag = NAM_wor_exile_war
			NAM_exile_return_programme_open = yes
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_war_of_return_ready_tt
				NAM_war_of_return_can_start = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 50
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			add_war_support = 0.05
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_war_of_return_start_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_exile_renew_army_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_war_of_return_start = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}
	}
}

		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 35
		days_re_enable = 60
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_completed_focus = NAM_hum_union_currency
						NAM_hum_government_operable = yes
						check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						FROM = {
							NAM_hum_district_workshop = yes
							NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_hum_union_order_paid }
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_place_union_order_result_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						hidden_trigger = {
							has_completed_focus = NAM_hum_common_market
							FROM = { NAM_hum_market_freight_available = yes }
						}
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_market_gun_order_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						FROM = {
							subtract_from_variable = { var = NAM_hum_market_lots value = 1 }
							set_state_flag = NAM_hum_order_reserved_lot
						}
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 300 }
					NAM_spend_treasury = yes
					FROM = { set_state_flag = NAM_hum_union_order_paid }
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_hum_government_operable = no
				FROM = { NAM_hum_district_workshop = no }
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_hum_government_operable = yes
						FROM = {
							NAM_hum_district_workshop = yes
							has_state_flag = NAM_hum_union_order_paid
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_union_order_delivery_tt
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { FROM = { has_state_flag = NAM_hum_order_reserved_lot } } }
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_market_gun_delivery_tt
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { NAM_hum_union_order_settle = yes } }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_union_order_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { NAM_hum_union_order_refund = yes } }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 8 }
	}

# After the exile war: Svetlogorye strengthens its hold on the returned
# south before the great war. The junta rules by garrisons and terror, the
# humanists by federal treaties and reconstruction.
NAM_consolidation = {
	# The great war ends only in full victory or in the annexation of
	# Svetlogorye, except when NAM_great_war_close_external closes it outside
	# the treaty and leaves Svetlogorye independent. The course's great war
	# focus is spent, so this decision declares the next one; it is visible
	# only in that case, because a won war sets NAM_great_war_won.
	NAM_renew_great_war = {
		icon = generic_prepare_civil_war
		allowed = { tag = NAM }
		visible = {
			NAM_consolidation_open = yes
			has_global_flag = NAM_great_war_started
			NOT = { NAM_great_war_active = yes }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = NAM_great_war_won }
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_great_war_consolidated_tt
				check_variable = { var = NAM_consolidation value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = NAM_great_war_ready_tt
				NAM_great_war_can_start = yes
			}
		}
		cost = 50
		fire_only_once = no
		complete_effect = {
			add_war_support = 0.05
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_great_war_start_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_great_war_start = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}
	}

				tooltip = NAM_board_harbour_cancel_tt
				OR = {
					NAM_country_operable = no
					AND = {
						700 = { has_state_flag = NAM_board_harbour_contract }
						NAM_board_harbour_works_operable = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						NAM_consolidation_open = yes
						has_country_flag = NAM_exile_humanist
						OR = {
							has_completed_focus = NAM_hum_harbour_rebuilding
							has_completed_focus = NAM_board_reconstruction_tenders
						}
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_board_reconstruction_tenders }
							NAM_board_harbour_works_operable = yes
						}
						NOT = { has_variable = NAM_hum_reconstruction_deposit }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_reconstruction_started_tt
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_completed_focus = NAM_board_reconstruction_tenders } }
					custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_board_harbour_works_tt
					hidden_effect = { 700 = { set_state_flag = NAM_board_harbour_contract } }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
					NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
					set_variable = { var = NAM_hum_reconstruction_deposit value = 60 }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = {
						has_variable = NAM_hum_reconstruction_deposit
						NAM_country_operable = yes
						OR = {
							700 = { NOT = { has_state_flag = NAM_board_harbour_contract } }
							NAM_board_harbour_works_operable = yes
						}
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_reconstruction_done_tt
				if = {
					limit = { hidden_trigger = { 700 = { has_state_flag = NAM_board_harbour_contract } } }
					700 = {
						NAM_board_complete_harbour_works = yes
						hidden_effect = { clr_state_flag = NAM_board_harbour_contract }
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					clear_variable = NAM_hum_reconstruction_deposit
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_cons_change value = 10 }
					NAM_consolidation_add = yes
				}
			}
			else = { hidden_effect = { NAM_hum_reconstruction_refund = yes } }
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_hum_reconstruction_refund_tt
			hidden_effect = { NAM_hum_reconstruction_refund = yes }
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 15
		}
	}
}

NAM_coalition_logistics = {
	NAM_coalition_transport_contract = {
		icon = ADISCORD_deal
		visible = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_coalition_mobilised }
		available = {
			has_capitulated = no
			has_tech = ADISCORD_tech_restored_rail_stock
			has_tech = ADISCORD_tech_restored_truck_fleets
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
		}
		cost = 50
		days_remove = 35
		days_re_enable = 60
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
		}
		cancel_trigger = { has_capitulated = yes }
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
					has_capitulated = no
				}
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 10 producer = ROOT }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = 120 producer = ROOT }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = 20 producer = ROOT }
				add_fuel = 20000
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid } }
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
				add_political_power = 50
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid } }
				hidden_effect = { clr_country_flag = NAM_rw_transport_contract_paid }
				add_political_power = 50
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_equipment = { train_equipment < 15 }
						has_equipment = { motorized_equipment < 100 }
						has_navy_size = { size < 40 type = convoy }
						has_fuel < 20000
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held } }
		}
		cost = 35
		days_remove = 60
		days_re_enable = 80
		modifier = {
			stability_weekly = 0.003
		}
		# Each running campaign holds its own slot receipt; every exit returns it once.
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_campaign_start_cooldown_80_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ADISCORD_has_campaign_slot = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held }
					}
					ADISCORD_campaign_slot_consume = yes
					set_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				NAM_colonial_programme_operable = no
				NAM_country_operable = no
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_campaign_cancel_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held }
					clr_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held
					ADISCORD_campaign_slot_release = yes
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held }
					clr_country_flag = NAM_campaign_every_seal_counts_slot_held
					ADISCORD_campaign_slot_release = yes
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_stability < 0.60
			}
		}
	}
